- **sacco-cross-team-collaboration**: Framework for effective cross-team communication via GitHub issues and PRs. - **sacco-parity-verification**: Contract test parity verification for system rewrites and technology migrations. Use when rewriting a system in a new language/framework, migrating between platforms, or ensuring API compatibility. Triggers: "parity", "contract tests", "ensure compatibility", "rewrite", "migration", "port from X to Y".
